- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared the proposed worker sigterm handling to the existing lifecycle structure in source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py:529-579. the queue entrypoint wraps startup and steady-state in one lifecycle block, but worker `main()` does not enter its cleanup `try/finally` until source/runtime/worker/server.py:405, so the proposed handler alone is not fully correct for startup-time shutdowns. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: compared the documented startup limitation to the actual lifecycle boundary in source/runtime/worker/server.py. because the cleanup try/finally still begins only at line 405, the proposed sigterm handler is only correct for steady-state shutdowns and not for startup-time sigterm, so the plan remains intentionally incomplete on that case. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /tmp/debug_logging_audit.py\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import json\n+import subprocess\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+REPO = Path([REDACTED])\n+PRIMARY_FILES = [\n+    \"source/models/wgp/param_resolution.py\",\n+    \"source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/join/final_stitch.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/join/generation.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/travel/segment_processor.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/travel/stitch.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/queue/task_processor.py\",\n+    \"source/task_handlers/travel/chaining.py\",\n+]\n+\n+\n+def count_debugs(path: str, text: str) -> int:\n+    return text.count(\".debug(\")\n+\n+\n+def head_text(path: str) -> str:\n+    proc = subprocess.run(\n+        [\"git\", \"show\", f\"HEAD:{path}\"],\n+        cwd=REPO,\n+        capture_output=True,\n+        text=True,\n+        check=True,\n+    )\n+    return proc.stdout\n+\n+\n+def main() -> None:\n+    counts = {}\n+    threshold_pass = True\n+    for rel in PRIMARY_FILES:\n+        current_text = (REPO / rel).read_text()\n+        current_count = count_debugs(rel, current_text)\n+        head_count = count_debugs(rel, head_text(rel))\n+        reduction = round((head_count - current_count) / head_count * 100, 1) if head_count else None\n+        counts[rel] = {\n+            \"head\": head_count,\n+            \"current\": current_count,\n+            \"reduction_pct\": reduction,\n+        }\n+        threshold_pass = threshold_pass and reduction is not None and reduction >= 40.0\n+\n+    report = {\n+        \"primary_files_meet_40pct\": threshold_pass,\n+        \"counts\": counts,\n+    }\n+    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True))\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    main()\n*** End Patch\n"
}
